IDENTITY AND POWER:
Frost is an adult white Bigfoot with blue eyes, upright humanoid limbs and broad hands and feet; never a dog, bear, human in a suit or cartoon. Use V08 for his exact powered markings, face and adult proportions. Part 254 explicitly transitions from C05 to V08 after all trials. From Part 255 onward the powered appearance remains consistent, even when its glow dims. No new costume, species or age changes.
Lily remains the same young adult mother matching V04, not pregnant or elderly. Tommy and Sarah remain reference-aged children across this batch and stay with guarded rear helpers during the battle; adult volunteers handle tunnel infiltration and fighting. Martha, Hank, Joe and Rose each keep their reference faces. Grace appears only in Part 265 at her old home.
Bruno is dead; V09 is a distinct translucent spirit, never a resurrected body. Orin is his separate elder father matching C06. Energy flows Orin into Bruno, then Bruno into Frost. Part 254 completes the gift prepared earlier, not a different source of power. Spirits fade after the written scene and do not become living army members.
Old Scar matches K08; its scar is a subtle healed detail, not a new creature design. Animal mouths remain natural; communication is through feeling, not human lip-synced words.

CONTINUITY AND COUNTS:
Time passes only where the plan states. Before the final battle do not show the king defeated. In the castle stable sequence there are three outer small dragons and three inner small dragons, six total once released. The mammoth charge uses one lead plus two additional mammoths, three total. Part 279 has exactly six dark Bigfoots and one T-rex. The great red dragon is a single consistent K02 creature with Magnus in a secured neck saddle, never another duplicate. Chains and the red crystal are separate controls: physical release does not instantly break all magic. Frost's brief setback in Part 285 is followed by recovery in Part 288, without a second awakening from rubble. The names room is discovered once in Part 287; later scenes handle its evidence. End on the final written image without a recap or unrelated copied cliffhanger.

NEGATIVE:
No subtitles, captions, logos, watermarks, random text, cartoon/anime or plastic faces, face swaps, extra limbs, duplicate children, dog-looking Bigfoot, gore, nudity, premature powers or unrequested speech. No baby Frost or sudden human aging. Keep spirit visions clearly separate from real geography.

Show only the characters called for by this half's shot plan; the repeated reference list also contains continuity guides. If the tool supports a starting-frame reference and the actual final frame of A is supplied, use it for B; otherwise reconstruct the written handoff, without claiming the final frame was loaded.

IDENTITY AND POWER:
Frost is an adult white Bigfoot with blue eyes, upright humanoid limbs and broad hands and feet; never a dog, bear, human in a suit or cartoon. Use V08 for his exact powered markings, face and adult proportions. Part 254 explicitly transitions from C05 to V08 after all trials. From Part 255 onward the powered appearance remains consistent, even when its glow dims. No new costume, species or age changes.
Lily remains the same young adult mother matching V04, not pregnant or elderly. Tommy and Sarah remain reference-aged children across this batch and stay with guarded rear helpers during the battle; adult volunteers handle tunnel infiltration and fighting. Martha, Hank, Joe and Rose each keep their reference faces. Grace appears only in Part 265 at her old home.
Bruno is dead; V09 is a distinct translucent spirit, never a resurrected body. Orin is his separate elder father matching C06. Energy flows Orin into Bruno, then Bruno into Frost. Part 254 completes the gift prepared earlier, not a different source of power. Spirits fade after the written scene and do not become living army members.
Old Scar matches K08; its scar is a subtle healed detail, not a new creature design. Animal mouths remain natural; communication is through feeling, not human lip-synced words.

CONTINUITY AND COUNTS:
Time passes only where the plan states. Before the final battle do not show the king defeated. In the castle stable sequence there are three outer small dragons and three inner small dragons, six total once released. The mammoth charge uses one lead plus two additional mammoths, three total. Part 279 has exactly six dark Bigfoots and one T-rex. The great red dragon is a single consistent K02 creature with Magnus in a secured neck saddle, never another duplicate. Chains and the red crystal are separate controls: physical release does not instantly break all magic. Frost's brief setback in Part 285 is followed by recovery in Part 288, without a second awakening from rubble. The names room is discovered once in Part 287; later scenes handle its evidence. End on the final written image without a recap or unrelated copied cliffhanger.

No baby Frost or sudden human aging. Keep spirit visions clearly separate from real geography.

IDENTITY AND POWER:
Frost is an adult white Bigfoot with blue eyes, upright humanoid limbs and broad hands and feet; never a dog, bear, human in a suit or cartoon. Use V08 for his exact powered markings, face and adult proportions. Part 254 explicitly transitions from C05 to V08 after all trials. From Part 255 onward the powered appearance remains consistent, even when its glow dims. No new costume, species or age changes.
Lily remains the same young adult mother matching V04, not pregnant or elderly. Tommy and Sarah remain reference-aged children across this batch and stay with guarded rear helpers during the battle; adult volunteers handle tunnel infiltration and fighting. Martha, Hank, Joe and Rose each keep their reference faces. Grace appears only in Part 265 at her old home.
Bruno is dead; V09 is a distinct translucent spirit, never a resurrected body. Orin is his separate elder father matching C06. Energy flows Orin into Bruno, then Bruno into Frost. Part 254 completes the gift prepared earlier, not a different source of power. Spirits fade after the written scene and do not become living army members.
Old Scar matches K08; its scar is a subtle healed detail, not a new creature design. Animal mouths remain natural; communication is through feeling, not human lip-synced words.

CONTINUITY AND COUNTS:
Time passes only where the plan states. Before the final battle do not show the king defeated. In the castle stable sequence there are three outer small dragons and three inner small dragons, six total once released. The mammoth charge uses one lead plus two additional mammoths, three total. Part 279 has exactly six dark Bigfoots and one T-rex. The great red dragon is a single consistent K02 creature with Magnus in a secured neck saddle, never another duplicate. Chains and the red crystal are separate controls: physical release does not instantly break all magic. Frost's brief setback in Part 285 is followed by recovery in Part 288, without a second awakening from rubble. The names room is discovered once in Part 287; later scenes handle its evidence. End on the final written image without a recap or unrelated copied cliffhanger.

No baby Frost or sudden human aging. Keep spirit visions clearly separate from real geography.
